commit 0a48f8c29785099b0450fb04051b0219d4535ce2
Author: Milos Vasic <i@mvasic.ru>
Date:   Thu Aug 6 04:27:54 2026 +0500

    fix(hxc-222): untrack 104MB of vendored node_modules (CONST-053)
    
    sdk/web/node_modules (7,870 files) and plugins/mcp-server/node_modules
    (1,037 files) were committed despite matching the repo's own
    node_modules/ .gitignore pattern (line 129) and, for the mcp-server
    tree, an even more specific plugins/mcp-server/node_modules/ pattern
    (line 57). Both were inert because git never applies ignore rules to
    paths already in the index (git rm --cached is required first) -
    confirmed with `git check-ignore --no-index` matching both paths and
    plain `git check-ignore` matching neither, before this change.
    
    Regeneration proven before removal: `npm ci` against each directory's
    committed package.json + package-lock.json, run from a clean scratch
    copy, reproduced both trees (378 packages / 7,870 files for sdk/web;
    19 packages / 1,037 tracked + 2 untracked bin files for mcp-server),
    exit 0, no network/auth issues (both manifests reference only public
    npm packages, no .npmrc/private registry anywhere in this repo).
    
    Bonus finding: the tree being removed was already broken. Every
    node_modules/.bin/* entry is a symlink to a <pkg>/bin/* target file
    (e.g. .bin/tsc -> ../typescript/bin/tsc), but no <pkg>/bin/* file was
    ever tracked in either directory - `tsc` was a dangling symlink and
    `npm run build` failed with "tsc: command not found" against the
    committed-then-checked-out tree. The npm-ci-regenerated tree has the
    real target files and a working tsc (verified: `tsc --version` ->
    5.9.3; sdk/web's own tsconfig.json compiles with real, pre-existing,
    out-of-scope TS diagnostics; plugins/mcp-server builds clean and both
    of its RED/GREEN guard tests pass end-to-end against the rebuilt
    dist/, which came out byte-identical to what was already committed).
    
    Both directories were already covered by .gitignore before this
    commit - no .gitignore edit was needed, only `git rm -r --cached`.
    
    git rm -r --cached staged exactly 8,907 deletions (7,870 + 1,037),
    all under the two target trees, nothing else - confirmed via
    `git status --porcelain` before and after, and a repo-wide grep of
    the staged set excluding those two pathspecs (0 hits) immediately
    before commit.
    
    Evidence: docs/qa/hxc222_node_modules_untrack_20260806T000000Z/ in
    the parent (helix_code) repo.
    
    Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
